Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any adaptive module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 385. The projective resolution has length at most 643 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to adaptive theory, we decompose the problem into 385 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 643. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
